# Feed parity matrix

Generated by `scripts/feed_corpus/parity_matrix.py` from the feed corpus
(`src-tauri/tests/fixtures/feed_corpus`); do not edit by hand. A cell lists the
corpus cases covering that broker, mode and depth, or its order updates, with
the worst result among them; what the web offers is each broker's
`broker.json`. A result holds when the case's test passes in CI. A deviation is
approved when it names the rule it follows (`approvals.json`). 1.0.7 ships when
`scripts/feed_corpus/release_gate.py --check` passes: every cell "matches web",
"approved deviation" or "not offered by the web", and every module builds its broker.

Cases: 445. Open gaps: 0. Deviations pending approval: 0.

| aliceblue | `absent_fields_and_a_price_without_time` | `ltt` | web-defect | `lost-data-kept` | The web sends AliceBlue's feed time ft unchanged as ltt: epoch seconds, and a string ("1794302700"); the protocol's WebSocket timestamps are epoch milliseconds (docs/prompt/websockets-format.md), so the unit is wrong, as for Dhan. The desktop sends the trade time in ms, as a number. |
| aliceblue | `absent_fields_and_a_price_without_time` | `average_price` | web-defect | `leave-out-unsent` | The web's Quote sends average_price 0.0, then 0, for a tick whose snapshot never carried ap (parsed_data.get("average_price", 0.0)); a value the broker did not send is left out, never sent as 0 (docs/api/websocket-streaming/quote.md). |
| aliceblue | `absent_fields_and_a_price_without_time` | `total_oi` | web-defect | `leave-out-unsent` | The web's Quote sends total_oi 0 for an equity whose ticks carry no toi; a value the broker did not send is left out, never sent as 0 (docs/api/websocket-streaming/quote.md). |
| aliceblue | `absent_fields_and_a_price_without_time` | `last_quantity` | web-defect | `lost-data-kept` | AliceBlue's tick carries last_quantity (ltq); the web's mapper never reads it (aliceblue_mapping.py:111-217), so its Quote leaves out a value the broker sent (lost data, lead ruling 2026-11-09). | arrow | `ltp_mode` | `close` | desktop-decision | `market-state-overlay` | Market-state rule (lead 2026-11-09: the previous close overlays in the snapshot). A 13-byte LTP packet carries no previous close. The bridge keeps the instrument's last known close within one connection (feed/bridge/snapshot.rs, audit C03) and works the change out again from the new price, so the frame carries the close and change the 17-byte packet brought; the web's frame from a 13-byte packet has none (the Arrow adapter keeps no snapshot). Nothing is invented: every value is the broker's. |

| compositedge | `index_quote_touchline` | `average_price` | web-defect | `per-packet-number-type` | With the 1501 quote nested in Touchline (the shape of this case) this web adapter reads 1501 at the message root ('data is at root level', :775) and sends average_price as 0: a wrong value, lost data. | definedge | `reconnect_resubscribes` | `average_price` | web-defect | `merge-restart-on-reconnect` | The web keeps the snapshot of the connection that dropped (_on_close and _resubscribe_all clear nothing) and publishes its average price as current with the first update after a reconnect; the desktop starts each connection's snapshot afresh (a cached market field belongs to the session that sent it). Lead decision 2026-11-09: restarting the merge after a reconnect is a desktop improvement, a web defect. |

| deltaexchange | `book_clear` | `average_price` | web-defect | `leave-out-unsent` | Delta's ticker never carries average price; the web sends a constant 0 (_normalise_ticker's defaults). The docs say leave out a field the stream does not carry, as the desktop does. |
| deltaexchange | `book_clear` | `oi_change` | web-defect | `leave-out-unsent` | Delta's ticker never carries an OI change; the web sends a constant 0 (_normalise_ticker's defaults). The docs say leave out a field the stream does not carry, as the desktop does. |
| deltaexchange | `book_clear` | `depth` | web-defect | `book-levels-as-sent` | The web pads a short or empty side of Delta's book to five zero levels Delta never sent; the desktop sends the levels Delta sent (an emptied side is an empty list). |
| deltaexchange | `book_then_ticker_depth` | `ltp` | desktop-decision | `per-broker-wire-shape` | A depth frame keeps ltp, which the SDK's Depth callback reads: 0 before the symbol's first ticker; the web's merged cache has no ltp until a ticker came. |
| deltaexchange | `book_then_ticker_depth` | `depth` | web-defect | `book-levels-as-sent` | The web pads a short side of Delta's book to five zero levels Delta never sent and gives them no order count; the desktop sends the levels Delta sent (lead rule on book levels: padding a web adapter adds is a web defect). |
| deltaexchange | `book_then_ticker_depth` | `average_price` | web-defect | `leave-out-unsent` | Delta's ticker carries no average price; the web sends a constant 0 (_normalise_ticker's defaults). The docs say leave out a field the stream does not carry, as the desktop does. |

| fivepaisa | `depth_before_quote` | `ltp` | desktop-decision | `per-broker-wire-shape` | A depth frame keeps ltp and timestamp before the instrument's first quote, for every broker (1.0.6 decision: the SDK's Depth callback reads them); the 5paisa web adapter leaves ltp out of a MarketDepthService frame that comes before any MarketFeedV3 frame (audit RB-04). The other quote fields are left out on both. |
| fivepaisa | `index_quote` | `last_quantity` | web-defect | `index-quote-zero-left-out` | 5paisa fills an index's traded quantity and book totals with 0 (an index has no trades and no book); the web passes the zeros on (_quote_fields feed.get("LastQty", 0), feed.get("TBidQ", 0)), the desktop's index frame leaves them out. Web sends zero; docs say leave out. |

Web sends zero; docs say leave out. |

| fivepaisa | `order_book_polling` | `frame` | web-defect | `never-copy-web-defect` | The web's 5paisa book (transform_order_data) has no filled_quantity, so the poller's (order_status, filled_quantity) never changes on a partial fill and the web publishes nothing for it; the desktop reads TradedQty and publishes the fill. |
| fivepaisa | `order_book_polling` | `filled_quantity` | web-defect | `never-copy-web-defect` | The web sends 0 for every 5paisa order (its book has no filled_quantity), a completed one included; the desktop sends TradedQty. |
| fivepaisa | `order_book_polling` | `pending_quantity` | web-defect | `never-copy-web-defect` | The web sends 0 for every 5paisa order (its book has no pending_quantity); the desktop sends PendingQty. |
| fivepaisa | `order_book_polling` | `average_price` | web-defect | `never-copy-web-defect` | The web sends 0.0 for every 5paisa order (its book has no average_price); the desktop sends AveragePrice. |
| fivepaisa | `order_book_polling` | `rejection_reason` | web-defect | `never-copy-web-defect` | The web's 5paisa book carries 5paisa's Reason as reason, and the poller reads rejection_reason, so a rejected order goes out with an empty reason; the desktop sends 5paisa's RMS text. Lost data on the web. |
| fivepaisa | `reconnect_after_held_prices` | `open` | web-defect | `merge-restart-on-reconnect` | The web keeps its snapshot across a reconnect (fivepaisa_adapter.py _on_open and _on_close leave last_snapshot), so a frame 5paisa sends after it with a price of 0 goes out with the pre-outage price as current; the desktop starts each connection afresh and leaves a price it does not have out (lead decision: restarting the merge after a reconnect is a desktop improvement). |

| fivepaisa | `sparse_feed_keeps_quantities` | `volume` | web-defect | `inherited-omissions` | 5paisa's MarketFeedV3 frame may repeat only what changed (here LastRate alone); the web reads every quantity it does not repeat as 0 (_quote_fields and _normalize_market_data: feed.get("TotalQty", 0), LastQty, TBidQ, TOffQ, BidQty, OffQty; only prices are held), so it sends invented zeros over the known quantities. The desktop keeps the last known value until 5paisa sends another, an explicit 0 included (review R107-08). Inherited web defect, approved by the lead's review-fixes decision. |

| fivepaisaxts | `index_quote_touchline` | `average_price` | web-defect | `per-packet-number-type` | With the 1501 quote nested in Touchline (the shape of this case) this web adapter reads 1501 at the message root ('data is at root level', :775) and sends average_price as 0: a wrong value, lost data. The desktop reads Touchline when the message has one, else the root, so it is right whichever shape XTS sends (quote_root and index_quote_root pin the root shape, which matches). Which shape a real 1501-json-full has is for the maintainer's market-hours check; this deviation holds when XTS nests the quote (lead ruling 2026-11-09). |

| fivepaisaxts | `quote_touchline` | `average_price` | web-defect | `per-packet-number-type` | With the 1501 quote nested in Touchline (the shape of this case) this web adapter reads 1501 at the message root ('data is at root level', :775) and sends average_price as 0: a wrong value, lost data. The desktop reads Touchline when the message has one, else the root, so it is right whichever shape XTS sends (quote_root and index_quote_root pin the root shape, which matches). Which shape a real 1501-json-full has is for the maintainer's market-hours check; this deviation holds when XTS nests the quote (lead ruling 2026-11-09). |

| fyers | `tbt_refused_serves_five` | `frame` | desktop-decision | `per-broker-wire-shape` | Fyers refused the 50-level socket, so the 50-level Depth subscription is served on the HSM depth feed's five levels at once (lead decision on the deep book); the web's oracle cannot model the refused TBT socket and publishes nothing for this packet (its adapter falls back to 5-level depth, fyers_websocket_adapter.py:373-381). |
| groww | `depth_book_price_and_clear` | `ltp` | desktop-decision | `per-broker-wire-shape` | A book before the first price: the web's merged entry has no ltp yet and leaves it out; the desktop's Depth frame keeps ltp (0) and timestamp, which the SDK's Depth callback reads (1.0.6 decision). |
| groww | `index_depth_falls_back_to_price` | `frame` | web-defect | `adapter-keys-carried` | The web's Groww adapter matches a Depth subscription on an index as LTP and publishes the tick on the LTP topic, which the proxy delivers only to LTP subscribers (server.py fans a topic out to its own mode and lower ones): the Depth client was acknowledged and never receives a price. The desktop sends it the index value at Depth, without a book. |
| groww | `order_book_polling` | `average_price` | web-defect | `adapter-keys-carried` | The web relays average_price 0.0 for a filled order: its Groww order book has no average fill price. The desktop sends Groww's average_fill_price. |
| groww | `order_book_polling` | `filled_quantity` | web-defect | `adapter-keys-carried` | The web relays filled_quantity 0 for a filled order: its Groww order book has no filled quantity. The desktop sends Groww's filled_quantity. |

| ibulls | `index_quote_touchline` | `average_price` | web-defect | `per-packet-number-type` | With the 1501 quote nested in Touchline (the shape of this case) this web adapter reads 1501 at the message root ('data is at root level', :775) and sends average_price as 0: a wrong value, lost data. The desktop reads Touchline when the message has one, else the root, so it is right whichever shape XTS sends (quote_root and index_quote_root pin the root shape, which matches). Which shape a real 1501-json-full has is for the maintainer's market-hours check; this deviation holds when XTS nests the quote (lead ruling 2026-11-09). |

| ibulls | `text_1105` | `frame` | web-defect | `never-copy-web-defect` | The web drops a 1105 text event: its client compares the stored string instrument id with an int, so no 1105 event passes its subscription filter, and its adapter drops a message without MessageCode as an unknown code. The desktop decodes the touchline its codes 110-119 carry (audit appendix, inherited item 4). |

| iifl | `index_quote_touchline` | `average_price` | web-defect | `per-packet-number-type` | With the 1501 quote nested in Touchline (the shape of this case) this web adapter reads 1501 at the message root ('data is at root level', :775) and sends average_price as 0: a wrong value, lost data. The desktop reads Touchline when the message has one, else the root, so it is right whichever shape XTS sends (quote_root and index_quote_root pin the root shape, which matches). Which shape a real 1501-json-full has is for the maintainer's market-hours check; this deviation holds when XTS nests the quote (lead ruling 2026-11-09). |

Which shape a real 1501-json-full has is for the maintainer's market-hours check; this deviation holds when XTS nests the quote (lead ruling 2026-11-09). |

| jainamxts | `index_quote_touchline` | `average_price` | web-defect | `per-packet-number-type` | With the 1501 quote nested in Touchline (the shape of this case) this web adapter reads 1501 at the message root ('data is at root level', :775) and sends average_price as 0: a wrong value, lost data. The desktop reads Touchline when the message has one, else the root, so it is right whichever shape XTS sends (quote_root and index_quote_root pin the root shape, which matches). Which shape a real 1501-json-full has is for the maintainer's market-hours check; this deviation holds when XTS nests the quote (lead ruling 2026-11-09). |

| jainamxts | `oi_event_1510_kept` | `missing-frame` | web-defect | `no-price-packet` | Jainam's adapter publishes the 1510 open-interest event and the 1505 candle to every held mode as market frames: a Depth frame with an LTP of 0 for each, the 1510's carrying only its OI right, the 1505's the one-minute bar's open, high, low and close as the day's. | jainamxts | `quote_touchline` | `average_price` | web-defect | `per-packet-number-type` | With the 1501 quote nested in Touchline (the shape of this case) this web adapter reads 1501 at the message root ('data is at root level', :775) and sends average_price as 0: a wrong value, lost data. The desktop reads Touchline when the message has one, else the root, so it is right whichever shape XTS sends (quote_root and index_quote_root pin the root shape, which matches). Which shape a real 1501-json-full has is for the maintainer's market-hours check; this deviation holds when XTS nests the quote (lead ruling 2026-11-09). |

A snapquote carries no trade price (packet_decoder.py:83-137, averageTradePrice at 126); the web publishes its average trade price as ltp (pocketful_adapter.py:676), so a client reads the day's average as the last trade. | rmoney | `unsubscribe_keeps_the_others` | `frame` | web-defect | `never-copy-web-defect` | This web adapter disconnects the whole socket after any unsubscribe (self.disconnect() in unsubscribe), so every other symbol stops streaming; the desktop removes only the instrument let go (audit appendix, inherited item 3). |
| rupeezy | `future_ltp_and_depth` | `close` | desktop-decision | `market-state-overlay` | Market-state rule (lead 2026-11-09: the previous close overlays in the snapshot). An ltp packet carries no previous close. The bridge keeps the instrument's last known close within one connection (feed/bridge/snapshot.rs, audit C03) and works the change out again from the new price, so the LTP frame carries the close and change the full packet brought; the web's frame from an ltp packet has none (the Rupeezy adapter keeps no snapshot). Every value is the broker's. |
| rupeezy | `future_ltp_and_depth` | `prev_close` | desktop-decision | `market-state-overlay` | Market-state rule (lead 2026-11-09: the previous close overlays in the snapshot). An ltp packet carries no previous close. The bridge keeps the instrument's last known close within one connection (feed/bridge/snapshot.rs, audit C03) and works the change out again from the new price, so the LTP frame carries the close and change the full packet brought; the web's frame from an ltp packet has none (the Rupeezy adapter keeps no snapshot). Every value is the broker's. |

| tradejini | `currency_quote_divisor` | `ltt` | web-defect | `alias-unit` | Tradejini sends the trade time in epoch seconds; the web formats it with the server's local time zone into a text such as "2025-11-05 14:25:31" (nxtradstream.py datefmt) and sends that as ltt, where feed timestamps are epoch milliseconds (docs/prompt/websockets-format.md). The desktop sends epoch ms, and leaves ltt out when Tradejini sent no trade time, where the web sends null (Quote, Depth) or "" (LTP). |

| tradesmart | `reconnect_starts_afresh` | `last_trade_time` | web-defect | `merge-restart-on-reconnect` | The web's Tradesmart adapter keeps its scrip cache across a reconnect (only Shoonya's _resubscribe_all clears it), so the first packet of the new connection, which has no trade time (ltt), goes out with the last connection's: a value from before the outage presented as current. The desktop starts each connection afresh. |

| upstox | `ltpc_ltp_and_index_quote` | `timestamp` | desktop-decision | `bk-a-final-approvals` | The web's Upstox LTP payload carries no timestamp; the docs' LTP frame does (docs/prompt/websockets-format.md, LTP: ltp and timestamp), and the docs rank above the adapter, so the desktop keeps it. |
| upstox | `reconnect_fresh_authorize` | `volume` | web-defect | `merge-restart-on-reconnect` | The web keeps Upstox's merged snapshot across a reconnect (_on_connect replays the subscriptions only), so its first packet after an outage publishes the old session's fields as current; the desktop starts the merge afresh (UpstoxFeed::on_connected), as CLAUDE.md requires (a cached market field belongs to the session that sent it) and the audit's regression matrix asks (reconnect with the first packet partial). Lead ruling 2026-11-09. |

| wisdom | `index_quote_touchline` | `average_price` | web-defect | `per-packet-number-type` | With the 1501 quote nested in Touchline (the shape of this case) this web adapter reads 1501 at the message root ('data is at root level', :775) and sends average_price as 0: a wrong value, lost data. The desktop reads Touchline when the message has one, else the root, so it is right whichever shape XTS sends (quote_root and index_quote_root pin the root shape, which matches). Which shape a real 1501-json-full has is for the maintainer's market-hours check; this deviation holds when XTS nests the quote (lead ruling 2026-11-09). |
